([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/benchmark-result-tabulation))
- **benchmark-result-tabulation**: Use when comparing isoform-assembly tools on the same dataset and you need a machine-readable, validated summary table (e.g. assembler vs. uniquely_assigned_isoforms) derived from IsoQuant assignment files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benchmark-result-tabulation--2ba240b027e66f1e))
- **benchmark-result-tabulation**: Use when timing a pipeline (e.g. scSPARKL on Spark) across multiple dataset sizes or executor/driver memory configurations and you need a per-subsample runtime table comparable to a sequential implementation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benchmark-result-tabulation--7035bf8a1e4fe59f))
- **benchmark-result-tabulation**: Use when comparing the computational cost (CPU time and wall-clock time) of pathogen-quantification tools for 10x single-cell data on one identical sample, to produce a ranked runtime table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benchmark-result-tabulation--afabcb9b072c5d7a))
- **benchmark-result-tabulation-csv**: Use when a study reports surrogate-model performance (ROC AUC, LOBOV validation AUC) and Platt-calibration quality (ECE/MCE/logLoss deltas) that must be reproduced as a structured, machine-readable results table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/benchmark-result-tabulation-csv))
- **benchmark-result-visualization**: Use when per-question model correctness data (e.g., GPT-4/GPT-3.5 responses scored against an ACG-SA answer key) must be aggregated into accuracy percentages and visualized as grouped bar charts matching a paper figure such as Fig. 1 or Fig. 7a. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/benchmark-result-visualization))
- **benchmark-result-visualization**: Use when ranked gene lists from a multi-tool benchmark (e.g. 305 patient cases, 308 causal genes spiked into 19 CGD genes) must be compared across tools and top-N thresholds via plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/benchmark-result-visualization))
- **benchmark-results**: Run the full AntHocNet benchmark loop — pre-flight-validate scenario configs, dispatch benchmark-cell runs and campaign plans, fetch results cheaply, check result plausibility and anchor floors, parse and A/B-compare runs, and summarize classified campaign CSVs — with automatic run-to-run-noise verdicts. Use whenever dispatching benchmark workflows, validating a scenario or its results, or collecting/comparing AntHocNet benchmark numbers (including results/campaign/*.csv sweeps) so validation, parsing, deltas, filtering, and the noise call are done by scripts instead of by hand in context. ([swarm-routing/AntHocNet](https://github.com/swarm-routing/AntHocNet/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/benchmark-results) · ★8)
- **benchmark-results**: Format evidence-backed performance results with only the software, dependency, device, browser, and physical environment dimensions relevant to the measured workload. ([nayounsang/younsang-codex-plugin](https://github.com/nayounsang/younsang-codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/write-pr-content/benchmark-results) · ★1)
- **benchmark-results-aggregation-and-visualization**: Use when a perturbation benchmark has produced per-regulator activity scores for multiple decoupleR methods and you must aggregate them into per-method performance metrics and ranking comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benchmark-results-aggregation-and-visualization))
- **benchmark-results-table-compilation**: Use when reproducing or auditing a paper's reported metrics and you need a machine-readable table of the proposed method's results plus every baseline row from its result tables (e.g., Tables 1 and 2 of the EVL-MCoT paper). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/benchmark-results-table-compilation))
- **benchmark-results-tabulation**: Use when comparing runtime, CPU usage, and peak memory of MAG taxonomy classifiers across the same benchmark datasets, especially when one tool's cost is dominated by reference loading (e.g. kMetaShot's ~22Gb HDF5 matrix requiring ≥32Gb RAM). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/benchmark-results-tabulation))
- **benchmark-results-tabulation**: Use when replacing a full SVD routine (e.g. base R svd) with a partial/truncated SVD (e.g. RSpectra::svds) inside an iterative algorithm like JIVE, and you need runtime evidence that the substitution preserves the computation while cutting cost. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/benchmark-results-tabulation))
